  "intent": "Determine whether the beliefs about being watched are fixed and unshakeable (delusional) or whether the patient has some doubt about them (potentially non-psychotic)",

It just... it just started happening.\n\nDoctor: During these 11 months, have there been any times — even a few days or a week — when the voice went away or the watching seemed to stop, or has it been pretty constant the whole time?\n\nPatient: I don't think so. It's just... it's been there the whole time, I guess. I don't really want to talk about that. I just... I hear her. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you been able to go to work and take care of your husband's daily needs during these 11 months, or has the voice and the watching made it hard for you to do those things?\n\nPatient: It's... it's been hard. My husband and our pastor, they've had to help out more... just to keep things going. I just... I can't keep up with it all right now.\n\nDoctor: Before these 11 months ago when the voice and watching started, have you ever had experiences like this in the past — hearing voices or feeling like people were watching or following you?\n\nPatient: No. I don't... And I still knew the agency was watching us. That hasn't stopped.\n\nDoctor: During those few weeks when things were quieter and you were sleeping more — during that time, did the voice completely go away, or was it still there but just quieter?\n\nPatient: No, it... it didn't go away.
